WHY ARE QUESTIONS SO IMPORTANT?

One thing I learned way later than I should've is the art
of asking great questions.

Most guys are not able to get women to open up simply
because they stick to the social niceties and never
scratch deeper than the surface.

The most tragic part is, Women are ready to open up.
They want to talk about themselves. But they never
hear the right questions.

Questions are great because they also get her to
qualify herself and make you the screener. How?

Because you are literally asking her to "entertain" you
in a way by opening up, and it gives you great
opportunities to pick up on things to go deeper on/
tease her/ disqualify her depending on you.

Just remember to not spit these questions one after
the other like a robot or an interviewer. You won't
need to use more than 2 per interaction, because you
will be going deeper into the specifics she tells you.

 In fact, in many cases, just 1 question can create a 1-
hour conversation if you both vibe and open up. It's a
life hack.

Lead the flow of the interaction and keep it as
organic as possible. Don't be in your head about
things. Remember, you're talking to an actual human
being and checking if you vibe and if she meets your
high standards.

To ask great questions, you must be a great listener.
You should pick up her subconscious cues and the
information she's dropping about herself which you
can later use to ask questions

Before we move onto the actual questions, there is
one KEY PRINCIPAL that you must remember-

To ask great questions, you
must be a great listener.

 You should pick up her subconscious cues and the
information she's dropping about herself which you
can later use to ask questions.

I'm not telling you to sit there and keep listening like
a good boy, but to actively do back and forths while
leading the conversation to where YOU  want it to go.

Stay on a topic for a few minutes, ask supplementary
questions, keep it organic, and give her strong
emotions to develop a deep and intense bond.

I |

What *NOT* to Ask

Firstly, let's talk about what kind of questions you
should NOT ask-

Never ask closed-ended or fact-finding questions.
These are boring and create an awkward silence after
she answers like "now what?"

Questions starting with:

Do-               Yes/No closed-ended
Who-           Fact-finding
Where-       Fact-finding
Were-         Yes/No

For example: Who is your favorite ____?
                             Do you know what happened in xyz ?

II |

The Kind of Questions you
Should Ask

you should ask charismatic, open-ended questions that
make her vulnerable.

These would be:

What
Why (great)
How
Would (it's about possibilities and finding their
edges)
Can- Yes, No but it's about commanding and
challenging the other person.

Don't be surprised if she says "No one's ever asked me
that before!"

Now, let's get into the exact questions and the
framework for asking these questions that make her
open up

1 |

"If you were to"

"If you were to go sleep with a guy on the first date and
you two had incredible sex, would you get attached?"

I really like this kind of question because it gives you
plausible deniability. You're not telling her she's going
to, but "IF YOU WERE TO"

It's a great question to ask her on the first date after
some rapport has been built by other questions.

This is because she has to imagine an orgasm and
having "incredible" sex, and the flow afterward.

With whom? she will associate those feelings with
you because you are there with her.

2 |

"At what Moment..."

At what moment did you decide...
At what moment did you realize ...

At what moment did you decide to end your previous
relationship and move on?

At what moment did you realize you like me so much?

I won't go into its importance.

But I will say this- your body will influence your
mind MUCH more than your mind will influence
your body.

In simple language-

When you portray a high-value and confident body
language, your brain automatically produces
positive thoughts, and the "Right Words" just come
out of your mouth. Sean Connery, pictured above,
is the perfect example of high-value body
language.

The first thing women will instantly recognize is
whether you have a high-status winner body
language or a low-value loser body language.

You will find a lot of gold below, but make sure you
practice it. Nothing comes naturally, and it WILL
feel awkward at first- that's OK.

With enough practice and fuck-ups, it will become
second nature man.

And that's all that truly matters.

1)  Stand up straight

 Your Chin should be just behind your crotch ( or belt-
front) It should be at a 45-degree angle from your neck,
looking straight.

2)  Feet

Your feet should be pointing towards her direction while
talking to her. If she feels uncomfortable, or if you want
to signal even higher value, you can move one foot away
from her.

3)  Progression

After your initial rapport building, move towards the
side, such that you both are looking at others. This will
create an "US VS THEM" dynamic. It's a great way to build
comfort. Literally, start talking about the environment
you're in, and some observations. She will agree with
you.

4) Shoulders

 Keep your shoulders back. Your Chest's shape should be
visible. No Slouching. Shoulders in front represent low
status.

5)  Hands

Your hands should not be in your pockets. Keep them by
your side.

If you want to keep your hand in your pocket, keep your
thumbs out. Your thumbs are your authority finger, and
should never be hidden.

6) Mirroring

 For instant rapport, mirror her body language, with a
slight delay so it doesn't seem obvious.

For example- if she puts her hand on her chin, you do
the same after a 5-second delay. It subconsciously
builds rapport.

7) Eye Contact

 Strong eye contact is a MUST.

When you have her eye contact, you have her attention.
Of course, look away every once in a while.

80% times eye contact /20% times not, is a good rule.

Pro Tip- stare into her soul when you drop the most
impactful word of your sentence.

8) BE OPEN & PRIDING

Have open body language.

You are a MAN. Never hide your body- take pride in it.
Keep your legs wide apart so that your crotch area is
visible to her.

She wants to see you being your dominant self.

9) POWER POSE

Find your power pose. This is your most uninhibited
self.

It's usually your arms outstretched, feet wide apart,
and looking upwards while closing your eyes and
thinking of a time you WON.

10) TAKING SPACE

Whenever you go to an event, make yourself
comfortable first.

Take up as much space as is required, provided there
was space, to begin with.

If there isn't space, take your time to find a good
spot. There's no hurry.

11) MOVEMENTS

Move slowly. Most Men walk fast, react fast, and just
don't take their time. Most Men also don't get laid.

Move slow, about 70% of your current speed. Practice
doing EVERYTHING at half speed for about 20 minutes
2-3 times a day, and that will drastically improve your
walk, movements, and body language.

Women notice these intricacies.

12) GROUNDED

Be grounded. Your feet should be wide apart, and your
center of gravity should be low. This ensures that you
are standing strong, which transcends into your voice
and gestures.

Nobody can fuck with you.

Pro-Tip-

At Clubs, Bars, and House Parties, hold your drink
outside the frame of your body (besides your body).
Guys who hold their drink in front of their stomach signal
under confidence, because it shows that you're trying to
protect yourself from external danger.

You're not most men. After reading and applying this
course, you will be in the top 1% of Men.

IS IT REALLY THAT SIMPLE?

 But the bottom line is, you need to become an

yourself about how better and more fulfilling life
becomes. As I spoke about in the "Mindset" Module
inside Social Circle Bible, we all are natural introverts.

You need to understand that the #1 reason you
IDENTITY as an introvert is because you grew up
watching things and being around people who made
being an introvert acceptable, and perhaps even "cool"

I fell for this myself. All those self-deprecating memes,
shows, movies, etc made me complacent.

Until I realized that's not how real life works. WAKE UP.

you watch this scene from the "Dark Knight Rises"

It applies to all things in life. You need to lose to ropes-

net that has prevented you from winning.

Trust me, after you do the following, especially the
"Quick Start Exercises" daily, you will realize that
you were an extrovert ALL ALONG. You just had to
reconnect with it.

Today is the first day of the rest of your life.

Expose yourself to Tension

You've become too comfortable in your own introverted
reality. You need to make Tension your best friend.

How? Do the things that make you uncomfortable at
best, and fucking terrified at worst.

The idea is to be in that situation for as long as you can.

cold showers & ice baths daily
weight lifting & increasing your load.
maintaining eye contact till the other person breaks
it.
not smiling or laughing in conversations. (extreme
for a while, then gradually smirk)

than your mind impacts your body.

The next time you feel sad or depressed, notice how
your body language is closed off, you're hunching over
and making yourself appear as "small" as you can.

Here are some great exercises to change your state
from introverted to extroverted:-

This will completely eliminate your programming and

Facial & Vocal Warm Up

1) 20-30 seconds:

Yawn widely to stretch the jaw, while making a sighing
sound for as long as you can exhale.

2) Tongue Stretches (60 seconds)

Stick the tongue out, point it up and down without
moving your jaw.
Then brace the tongue behind the front bottom teeth
and push the center against the inside of your teeth.

(last 20 seconds) Massage the muscles of the jaw and
face to further reduce tension. Stretch the neck lightly
by rotating it.

3) For 20 seconds, close your eyes, while making a

Maintain this sound steadily for as long as you can.
This helps clear and even out your voice while

4) For the next 60 seconds, pick out anything from
your environment (the view, the atmosphere,
anything) and talk about it.

There are no standards here- you can talk with
yourself about anything related to that object. Be
completely free-flowing with 0 judgment.

If you want, look up some tongue twisters to warm

AN IMPORTANT STORY

Once there was a farmer, with two sons.

 One day, one of his sons came back with a
wild horse he'd found.

"Wow, you're so lucky!" said his neighbors.
"Maybe," Said the farmer.

The next day, his son was taming and
training the horse, when he fell and broke
his arm, which meant he wouldn't be able to
help with the farming.

"Wow, that's unlucky!" said the neighbors.
"Maybe," said the farmer.

The Lesson here?

An Event can have INFINITE number of
meanings, depending on the circumstances.
 That is where YOUR FRAME comes in. How you
see events will decide your outcomes.

There's a great saying-

"Events are just events. It is we who frame them
as positive or negative"

With that, Let's begin.

WHAT IS FRAME?

The above story should give you a basic
idea about what frame is. Let me make it
clear here.

Frame is your perception of reality. It is how
you interpret events, things, people, etc.

The concept of frame is important because
reality is always malleable and subject to
interpretation.

Whoever is more convinced of their
interpretation will influence the behavior of
everyone around them.

Whoever has the stronger frame "controls"
how everybody interprets events.

THE TWO FRAMES
When going to meet a girl, your frame can either be

“God, I hope she finds me attractive “

Or it can also be

“God, I hope I find her attractive”

nervous, chasing- SHE WILL SCREEN YOU

And in the second situation, you will be calm, you will
maintain strong eye contact, you will tease and
disqualify her, and most importantly- YOU WILL
SCREEN HER

screening women

Nobody likes a loser. No, we support the underdogs
who bounce back harder and stronger. Reframing
allows you to do exactly that.

The best part?

After a couple of months of practice, you will
automatically reframe every event positively and in an
instant- without even thinking about it

Sometimes, however, reframes don't work- we discuss
why under "Box Framing"

BIG

BOX FRAMING

By far the strongest, and extremely important
for conversational dominance- with both men
and women.

You put their frame in a smaller box and wrap
your frame in a bigger box that carrier their
smaller frame

This way, you’re not denying or opposing their
frame. You’re simply acknowledging their frame
but also implying that your frame is an obvious
and stronger one.

Example- she says that she has a boyfriend.

Reframing won’t work here, you can’t say
“She’s just saying that to play hard” (She
could be, but the odds are less)

The better way is to put her frame in a small
box.

Let’s say she’s 5’6

Here’s how you do it- “Woah, easy tiger, I
barely know your name. Besides, I only date
girls who are 5’8 or taller”

What you’ve done is you’ve acknowledged
the fact that she has a boyfriend

WHAT ARE METAPHORS AND WHY
SHOULD YOU LEARN THEM?

As you already know, I preach ALL AROUND GROWTH. You don't want to be a
"Pickup Artist" who has all the lines but no charisma and character to back
them up with substance.

There's a saying-

If you want to change the world, change the metaphor.

And this holds a lot of truth.

Have there been times when you wanted to get a point across to someone,
but you simply couldn't by using explanations?

That's exactly where metaphors steps in.

Why do metaphors work so well? Because we've always tried to explain
things by comparing them to something else.

For example, someone asks you-

"Why do you read so many books?"

You could say- "It teaches me a lot, It's very calming, I like to stay on top of
things. blah blah blah."

Or, you could simply respond-

"Because Reading is to the mind, what exercise is to the body.

BOOM.

Which one had a greater effect on you? That's why learning metaphors is
CRUCIAL to becoming a high-value man.

"Metaphors are to a High-Value Man, what a sharpened spear is to a skilled
warrior."

O1 The cat that was actually

a dog

decisions are influenced by preceding thoughts.

If Person A goes first and votes "yes" for a cause, it is likely
that the next person will also vote "yes"; and before you
know it, the whole room votes yes.

we are primed to say that. Nobody wants to feel left out of
the herd. sequential order changes decisions.

This is why so many great ideas die. People don't stand up.

99% don't. Realize this, and be the odd one that says "NO".

You will be suitably rewarded, since your idea will be taken
up, and you will have become a leader. Most likely the
people who vote after you will mimic your vote.

People are extremely self-conscious.

 In fact, there were studies conducted where if you showed a
group picture of a dog, and the first person says it's a cat, the
next person has an 85% chance of agreeing that it's a cat
even when it's obvious it's a dog.

O2 First Impression stay, but

Last impressions Last.

You might have heard, first impressions last. well, it turns
out that last impressions last too.

It was found that people who went to a crowded
amusement park, with long waiting lines, unhygienic
environments, and crying kids did not in fact seem to care
about it, if the last few rides were pleasant, with no waiting
lines, clean surroundings, and a peaceful atmosphere.

This is also called the "Peak end effect"

There is power in this.

Say, you are going to give a presentation to your boss, or
to a board that you want to impress. There are many
others going for the role as well. Ideally, you want to go
either first, or preferably last. This is because people
remember only the very first and the most recent ones.

Think of your dating life. you surely remember the first
women you slept with, the whole experience, the
afterglow, etc.

 Then there were many blurred memories in between, a lot
of blanks that you can't seem to fill. You do, however, also
remember the most recent woman you slept with. That is
the power of recency.

Back to the interview.

In Business, It was found that you are
60% more likely to be selected for a job/
promotion when you were the last person
to give the interview or the presentation.
That means, even if the person preceding
you was much better than you, you are
more likely to get promoted just because
you were the last, and the one that they
remembered!

In Dating- If you are texting with a girl, be
sure to send her a meme, or something
witty and flirty right around the time she
sleeps. She will remember you, and tend
to hit you up when she wants someone to
"console" her ;)

SMOOTH OPERATOR:

# CLOSE & DATE SETUP

When it has been understood that there's a low
chance of sleeping with her that night, you will
have to keep her in your funnel by getting her
number and setting up a date instead.

This gives her a chance to convey any remaining
concerns and also removes the pressure off her.

Tell her " It's a bummer that you can't come
tonight, but let's meet later this week. Is Tuesday
better for you or Thursday?

You're thus giving her the illusion of Option. She
has to choose from 2 options, which reduces the
chances of her saying she's busy both days.

Select the date, location, and time. Pizza, Coffee,
or Ice cream all work great. Which girl says no to
Ice Cream?

The location should be close to your place, or the
hotel. "Cool. There's this crazy place called __.
Let's meet around (time). "

Once the date is set up, take her phone and save
your number as "(Name) My future Ex Boyfriend."
or "Mr. Handsome" or any inside joke that you two
shared.

Then, text yourself from her phone, something
playful that when she looks at the next morning,
she remembers that feeling.

"Hey, it's Laura. I can't wait to see you on Tuesday
at Jamie's at 8 pm. I'll wear something tight ;)"

This is, of course, for the girls you cold
approach/Instagram. You don't need to go that
hard with girls you meet via social circle- but the
added fun element is always good.

This being done, start this with the next girl- don't
waste more time on her.

If she doesn't give her number/instagram, don't
try. Move on- plenty of fish in the sea.

If she's not sure about her schedule, be cool about
it and set a day, but "no worries if we have to
change it"

It's important to be nonchalant about it- it shows
that you have multiple options and a lot more to
life than going to a date with her.

A few hours before the date, confirm her presence
by building anticipation and future pacing-

"You're not one of those lightweight girls who can't
handle her booze, are you? I'll see you at 8pm"

The #1 Reason Women Flake is because they're in
a bad state *in that moment* By leading with that
tease, you're creating a challenge for her!

Once you're on the date, and this is the most
important no matter if you know NOTHING about
her.

Break the ice immediately by breaking rapport
with her. The Bible of Spark is extremely useful for
this.

Why? Because what kind of people break rapport
with each other comfortably? People who've
known each other for AGES.

Another thing, always sit BESIDE a girl. Never
opposite. sitting facing the same way Creates an
"us vs them" frame. Trust me on this.

Once you're there, the goal should be to give an
emotional rollercoaster (the friendzone
destruction bible), build comfort (charismatic
questions), have fun (word-for-word attraction),
and then either take her home OR leave at a high
and plan a next date. You can tell which by the way
she responds to your game!

WHAT IS RAPPORT?

Rapport is a relation of relatability.

 It’s a situation when you both have a connection of any
topic and can relate to one another.

 If you get introduced to a girl by your homie, you

common connect- YOUR HOMIE.

When you add TRUST to RAPPORT, you create
COMFORT.

TRUST + RAPPORT = COMFORT

The girl you met has rapport with you without speaking
a single word out, but there might not be comfort built
yet.

SMALL HOOPS

Think of Hoops are those Circles of Fire that Lions
have to jump through at the circus to get the meat
as a reward.

 No jump = no reward.

A small qualification hoop is a question that only
requires a yes/no answer. It’s to test the waters.

Example- “Do you party?” Notice how these are
short, direct, to the point. You’re not asking her if
she “likes to” party, you’re simply giving her a
choice.

If she gives a simple “yes/no” answer, you haven’t
teased (breaking rapport) her enough because a
girl who’s invested will NEVER give yes/no
answers.

What is the purpose of a shit test and why
should you even care about it?

A woman shit tests a man only when she is
attracted to him.

Observing your reaction to the shit test helps
her assess his sexual market value (SMV)
relative to hers.

Men can pass shit tests if they identify them
quickly and respond to them appropriately.

However, most men are unable to even
recognize a shit test until it is too late.

The average man must learn to identify and
deal with shit tests quickly because failing a
series of shit tests can dramatically lower his
perceived SMV in the eyes of the woman who
shit tested him.

If the drop in his perceived SMV is low enough
for her to think that her SMV is greater than
his SMV, she will either unhappily settle for a
man whom she thinks is not good enough for
her or set her sights on other men with higher
SMVs.

THE ESSENCE OF
SHIT TESTS

I don't understand why guys hear a shit
test and think it's a bad thing. If anything,
if she isn't throwing shit tests at you, you
should be concerned.

Because if she doesn't give a fuck about
you, she won't even bother to test you.

interest and investment in you. When you

sleeping with you.

KEY CONCEPTS

#1)  CHALLENGE

Remember to come across as playful
when you say this. It shows that you're not
looking for her approval, and are not
scared to challenge her to rise to your
level.

Ex- She says you're too old/too going for
her

You reply- "too old or can't handle?"

#2)  SELF DEPRECATION-

These should be used when you have a
clear high value in that environment, and
she knows it.

If you do, it shows insane confidence,
self-validation, and security in your own
skin. She knows you're not taking her
seriously and she feels totally one-upped.

#3)  AGREE AND EXAGGERATE:

This is a classic technique to ace her shit
tests. What you do is you make her
statement sound ridiculous to the extent
where she knows that you're a veteran at
the game.

Ex- She asks you if that's your pickup line

Your reply: "Yeah, and I practice it
everyday first thing after waking up"

#4)  DISAGREE AND EXAGGERATE:

Basically the opposite of the above, but
works just as well.

She asks if that's your pickup line

Your reply: "No, actually you're the first
girl I've ever spoken to"

#5)  DOMINATING:

This works extremely well when you are
jacked, handsome, and dressed perfectly
for the occasion. She's obviously ready to
sleep with you, and you can tell by the
signs she's throwing your way.

For example, she says "Are you hitting on
me?"

Your reply- "Yes, yes I am." with an
assertive tone, intense eye contact and
High-Value body language.

#6)  FLIPPING IT BACK ON HER:

Here you get out of the accusation by
instead reversing it on her.

example- "she says, Sorry, I have a
boyfriend"

Your response- "Woah, easy tiger. I came
over to tell you that you reminded me of
my little sister"

#7)  IGNORE:

This works especially well if you are in a
social event because you can ignore her
and instead talk to the other girls around
there.

The example here is simple. She says
something, you completely ignore and
instead start gaming her female friend.

#8)  MISINTERPRET:

This one is too fucking funny and you can
effectively interpret it into anything. You
shake her off her frame and now she has
to qualify herself by explaining that she
meant something else

For example- she says "do you say that to
every girl?"

You reply- "Did you just say you work at
McDonald's?"

#9)  TREAT HER LIKE A 10 YEAR
OLD

Here, you simply react the same way you
would if a 10-year-old tried to act smart in
front of you, you could effectively play
dumb to go down to her level.

Example- She says: " Is that your pickup
line?"

You- " What line?"

WHY SOCIAL CIRCLE?

Because women desire men whom other women
desire and other men admire.

Feminine energy is attracted to Masculine energy.

And nothing oozes more masculine energy than a
man who already has multiple women around him
chasing his attention and men who respect his
opinions and look up to him for advice and fun.

Simply put, pussy and power attract more pussy.

But on a deeper level, social circles are a lifestyle
thing. The power of introductions are very strong
due to the Halo effect, and in-group bias.

It's about Leverage. You leverage your access to
social resources and women to develop deeper
relationships with men and women.

THE MINDSET: I AM AN
 EXTROVERT

Look, everybody is a natural introvert. Nobody came out
of the womb ready to socialize and talk to people.  As
humans, we like to have alone time for ourselves.

be excited about.

deeper the connection with be.

connections.

Your Most powerful weapon

When you move to a new city, you need to realize that

Always tip heavy.

When you move to a new city, explore the local bars,
pubs, restaurants alone. Right after you order, tip then
and there and small talk with them about the location,

Do the same with bouncers and gatekeepers in clubs

instead of when you arrive.

 Why? Because 99% of people tip them before in an

destroys any chance of real rapport or relationship
building.

Instead, tip when you leave and do some chit-chat, ask
exchange names and compliment them for doing such
a great job and thank them. This will make them
remember you and after a couple of nights of this, you
will never have to wait in line again. Imagine how wet
the girls in line will be when they see you do this.

people do in life.
